<!-- laws: laws 75-81, 92-96, 245-250, 251-325 -->
- **Confirmation:** ❌
- **Problem:**
  1. `backend/Dockerfile.prod:28` No STOPSIGNAL, no pre-stop hook, no graceful shutdown config (cited line drifted; agent confirmed at 1-26) — claim accuracy: partial (`D2P-DOCKER-006`, `13_dev_to_prod_docker_verify`)
  2. `backend/Dockerfile.prod:28` Gunicorn runs without --workers flag (defaults to 1) (cited line drifted; agent confirmed at 26) (`D2P-DOCKER-011`, `13_dev_to_prod_docker_verify`)
  3. `backend/Dockerfile:1-32, backend/Dockerfile.prod:1-28` No multi-stage build; copies entire source including __pycache__, .venv, dev files (cited line drifted; agent confirmed at backend/Dockerfile:1-65, backend/Dockerfile.prod:1-26) — claim accuracy: partial (`D2P-DOCKER-012`, `13_dev_to_prod_docker_verify`)
  4. `backend/Dockerfile:1-32, backend/Dockerfile.prod:1-28` No PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 env var; no HEALTHCHECK instruction (cited line drifted; agent confirmed at backend/Dockerfile:1-65, backend/Dockerfile.prod:1-26) (`D2P-DOCKER-013`, `13_dev_to_prod_docker_verify`)
- **Solution:**
  1. Add STOPSIGNAL SIGTERM, ensure Gunicorn graceful timeout, add pre-stop hook in Coolify — verify: docker stop <container> triggers graceful shutdown — test: tests/infrastructure/test_graceful_shutdown.py — fields filled by agent: Actual_line_range, File:Line
  2. Add --workers 4 (or env-configurable) to Gunicorn command — verify: docker exec <container> ps aux — test: grep gunicorn — fields filled by agent: Actual_line_range, Evidence strength, File:Line
  3. Refactor to multi-stage: builder stage installs deps, runner stage copies only installed package + code — verify: docker images shows smaller backend image — test: tests/infrastructure/test_image_size.py — fields filled by agent: Actual_line_range, File:Line
  4. Add ENV PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 and HEALTHCHECK CMD curl -f http://localhost:8000/health — verify: VERIFIED — test: docker-compose.yml:56-61 (healthcheck in compose) — fields filled by agent: Actual_line_range, Blast radius, Blocks, Claim state, Completion blocker, Confidence

- **Confirmation:** ❌
- **Problem:**
  1. `backend/infrastructure/observability/error_handler.py:191-215; frontend/web_app/src/lib/api/errors.ts:8-22` Backend global handler returns RFC 7807 Problem Details: `{type, title, status, detail, instance, request_id, timestamp, errors}`. Frontend `getErrorMessage` only checks `data.detail` (string), `data.detail[]` (array), `data.message`, `data.error` (string). Frontend does not parse `type`, `title`, `instance`, `request_id`, `timestamp`. (`ALIGN-006`, `26_code_alignment`)
- **Solution:**
  1. Update `getErrorMessage` to read `data.detail` (RFC 7807 compliant); update `handleApiError` to log `request_id`, `type`, `timestamp` from RFC 7807 response — verify: Compare frontend error extraction with backend error shape — test: tests/frontend/web_app/test_error_handling.test.ts — fields filled by agent: Test, Verify
